# Skills · E · part 93

803 entries, experiment to experiment-rigor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **experiment**: Settle a hypothesis with a small, pre-registered experiment run by subagents in a sandbox, graded by a script, and reported as SUPPORTED, REFUTED or INCONCLUSIVE with n. Use proactively whenever a claim, plan or answer rests on something nobody has checked and a quick safe test could check it, and when D says "prove it", "are you sure", "verify that", "test the hypothesis", "I'm not sure", "we're guessing", or a claim would otherwise be marked untested or inference. Not for running a project's own checks or test suite (/verify), or for vetting an argument (/colorwheel).
